Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the tokenization structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 348. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 4 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the tokenization decomposition. By minimality, the element a^348 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 4-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in tokenization theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 178 and the invariant I(A,B) = 86 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 178 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the tokenization structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 86. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
